L1451: Line graph showing Mean Lymphocyte Count (10^3 cells/µL) over time for Teplizumab (red line) and Placebo (blue line) groups in the TN-10 study. The Y-axis ranges from 0.0 to 2.5. The X-axis shows visits from Baseline to Visit 27 (66 Months). Both groups start around 1.9-2.0. Teplizumab shows a significant decline starting around Visit 1 (Day 1), reaching a nadir of approximately 0.5 at Visit 6 (Day 6), and then recovering to baseline levels by Visit 11 (Day 11). Placebo levels remain relatively stable around 1.9-2.0 throughout the study.
L1452: Box plot showing Maximum ALT (Multiples of ULN) on a semi-log scale across different time periods (Baseline, During Cycle, Post-Cycle Weeks 1-2, Post-Cycle Weeks 3-6, Post-Cycle Weeks 7-14) for three treatment arms: Control (red), Teplizumab (teal), and ARM (blue). The Y-axis is logarithmic, ranging from 0.1 to 10.0. The plot shows that ALT levels are generally low (below 1.0x ULN) across all time points and arms, except for a few outliers. During the 'During Cycle' period, the Teplizumab arm shows a higher median and IQR compared to Control and ARM, with several outliers reaching 5x ULN (around 5.0) and 10x ULN (around 10.0). After the cycle, levels drop significantly for all arms, with Teplizumab showing the lowest median and IQR.
L1453: Line graph showing Mean Hemoglobin (g/dL) by Visit and Treatment Arm (Full ISS). The X-axis represents visits (Baseline, Day 1, Day 2, Day 3, Day 4, Day 5, Day 6, Day 7, Day 9, Day 11, Day 13, Week 1-2, Week 3-6). The Y-axis represents Hemoglobin (g/dL), ranging from 12.9 to 13.6. Two lines are plotted: Teplizumab (red) and Control (blue), both with error bars. Both groups start around 13.5 g/dL at Baseline. Both groups show a decline in hemoglobin starting around Day 5, reaching a nadir around Day 7 (Teplizumab around 13.0 g/dL, Control around 13.1 g/dL). Both groups recover significantly by Week 3-6, reaching levels slightly above 13.5 g/dL.
L1454: Figure 26 is a line graph titled "Mean Platelet Count by Visit and Treatment Arm (ISS)". The Y-axis represents Platelets (10^3 cells/\muL), ranging from 210 to 280. The X-axis represents Visit, spanning from Baseline to Week 35-38. Two treatment arms are compared: Teplizumab (red line) and Control (blue line). Both groups show a significant decline in platelet count starting around Day 1, reaching a nadir around Day 5 (Control) and Day 6 (Teplizumab), where counts dropped below 220 10^3 cells/\muL. Following this nadir, both groups show a recovery phase, with counts rising sharply by Day 11. By Week 1-2, both groups stabilize around 270 10^3 cells/\muL. Teplizumab generally maintains slightly higher platelet counts than the Control group during the recovery phase, although the Control group shows a sharp decline around Week 3-6, dropping below 240 10^3 cells/\muL, before recovering by Week 11-14.
L1455: Figure 27 is a line graph titled "Mean PT/INR by Visit and Treatment Arm (TN-10)". The Y-axis represents Prothrombin International Normalized Ratio (%), ranging from 100 to 115. The X-axis represents Visit, spanning from Baseline to Visit 11. Two treatment arms are compared: Teplizumab (red line) and Placebo (blue line). Both groups start near 105% at Baseline. Teplizumab shows a sharp increase in PT/INR starting around Visit 5 (Day 5), peaking significantly at Visit 6 (Day 6) around 113%, before declining. The Placebo group remains relatively stable, fluctuating slightly around 105% throughout the study period.
L1456: Figure 28 is a line graph titled "Mean Calcium (mg/dL) by Visit and Treatment Arm (ISS)". The Y-axis represents Calcium (mg/dL) ranging from 9.0 to 9.7. The X-axis represents Visit, including Baseline, Day 1 through Day 11, Day 13, Week 1-2, Week 3-6, Week 11-14, and Week 19-22. The graph compares two treatment arms: Teplizumab (red line) and Control (blue line), both with error bars.
At Baseline, both arms show calcium levels around 9.3 mg/dL. During the dosing period (Day 1 to Day 11), the Teplizumab arm shows a significant decrease in mean calcium, reaching a nadir around Day 5 (approximately 9.05 mg/dL), while the Control arm remains relatively stable (around 9.25-9.3 mg/dL). Both arms recover significantly by Week 1-2 (around 9.55 mg/dL) and maintain high levels throughout the follow-up period (Week 3-6 to Week 19-22).
The legend indicates: Teplizumab (Red line), Control (Blue line), and Teplizumab (Red line with error bars).
